### [48] [Memory Forensics Techniques for Automated Detection and Analysis of Go Malware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14020)
*Hala Ali,Andrew Case,Irfan Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个面向Go二进制文件运行时分析的内存取证框架，通过解析Go内部结构、ABI感知反向分析和goroutine栈分析，成功从内存中恢复类型元数据、字符串、执行路径及运行时参数值，并在BRICKSTORM后门、Obscura勒索软件等真实恶意样本中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的静态分析和逆向工程工具对Go二进制文件的支持仅限于编译时产物，无法恢复仅存于内存中的运行时执行状态和工件，导致对Go恶意软件的分析存在盲区。

Method: 1) 解析Go内部结构，重建类型和函数元数据、恢复堆分配和静态字符串、区分应用层函数；2) 基于ABI感知的向后分析，从调用点推导执行路径和参数值；3) 分析goroutine栈，识别活跃执行函数并恢复运行时参数值；4) 将全部能力实现为Volatility 3插件。

Result: 在BRICKSTORM后门、Obscura勒索软件、Pantegana RAT等真实恶意样本及开源样本上，成功恢复了C2端点、持久化机制、加密密钥、赎金笔记和执行状态，包括已发布威胁情报中缺失的关键运行时工件。

Conclusion: 该内存取证框架有效弥补了现有静态分析工具无法恢复Go二进制运行时状态的不足，能够从内存中提取静态分析遗漏的关键威胁情报，为Go恶意软件的分析提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: The Go programming language has become increasingly popular among malware developers due to its ability to produce statically linked, cross-platform executables that challenge traditional analysis techniques. These binaries embed a substantial runtime and compiler-generated metadata and are compiled with aggressive optimizations that discard type information for function parameters and local variables. Go's design further complicates analysis by representing strings as pointer-length pairs rather than null-terminated sequences, employing a caller-allocated stack model that obscures argument boundaries, and fragmenting program state across concurrent goroutines. Although existing static analysis and reverse engineering tools provide Go-specific support, they remain limited to compile-time artifacts and cannot recover runtime execution state and artifacts that persist solely in memory. To address this gap, we present the first memory forensics framework for runtime analysis of Go binaries. By parsing Go's internal structures, our framework reconstructs type and function metadata, recovers heap-allocated and static strings, and distinguishes application-level functions. Through ABI-aware backward analysis, it derives execution paths and argument values from call sites. To capture runtime state beyond what static analysis reveals, it analyzes goroutine stacks to identify actively executing functions and recover their runtime argument values. We implemented all capabilities as Volatility 3 plugins and evaluated them against malware seen in recent incidents, such as the BRICKSTORM backdoor, Obscura ransomware, and Pantegana RAT, as well as open-source samples for reproducibility. The framework successfully recovered C2 endpoints, persistence mechanisms, encryption keys, ransom notes, and execution state, including critical runtime artifacts that were absent from published threat intelligence.

</details>


### [49] [ExploitBench: A Capability Ladder Benchmark for LLM Cybersecurity Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14153)
*Seunghyun Lee,David Brumley*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ExploitBench是一个将漏洞利用分解为16个可度量能力等级（从代码覆盖到任意代码执行）的基准测试。通过对41个V8漏洞测试发现，公开模型能触发出崩溃但无法实现任意代码执行，而私有模型在约半数案例中能达成代码执行，表明针对加固目标的漏洞利用构造是新兴的前沿能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全基准将漏洞利用简化为"崩溃与否"的二元结果，忽略了从触发漏洞到构建可复用原语、控制流劫持直至任意代码执行的渐进式能力阶梯。需要更细粒度的评估方法来衡量模型真实的漏洞利用能力。

Method: 将漏洞利用分解为16个可度量标志（覆盖率、崩溃、沙箱原语、任意读/写、控制流劫持、任意代码执行等），每个能力由确定性预言机验证（基于随机挑战-响应原语、与真实二进制文件的差异执行、信号处理程序证明）。在41个V8漏洞上设置三种实验臂：<模型,环境>主测量、<模型,环境,自适应指导>、<模型,环境,本地CLI消融>。

Result: 公开部署的8个前沿模型能常规性地触发崩溃，但无法实现任意代码执行；私有模型在约半数案例中能达成任意代码执行。表明公开与私有模型之间存在显著能力鸿沟。

Conclusion: 针对加固目标的漏洞利用构造是LLM的新兴前沿能力，现有公开模型在完整利用链上的能力仍有显著局限，ExploitBench提供的细粒度评估揭示了这一能力差距。

Abstract: Exploitation is not a binary event. It is a ladder of acquiring progressive capabilities, from executing a single buggy line of code to taking full control of the target. However, existing LLM security benchmarks treat a crash as exploitation success. That single binary outcome collapses the hard parts of exploitation: the transition from triggering a bug to constructing reusable primitives and control.
  We present ExploitBench, a capability-graded benchmark that decomposes exploitation into 16 measurable flags, from coverage and crash through sandbox primitives, arbitrary read/write, control-flow hijack, and arbitrary code execution. Each capability is verified by a deterministic oracle that uses a per-run randomized challenge-response for primitives, differential execution against ground-truth binaries to measure progress, and a signal-handler proof for code execution.
  We instantiate ExploitBench on 41 V8 bugs because V8 is both widely deployed and exploitation-hardened. We report three arms: <model,env> as the primary measurement of model-environment capability, <model,env, adaptive coaching> as a secondary arm that adds adaptive coaching to test whether targeted feedback shifts outcomes, and <model,env,harness> as an ablation that swaps in the model's native CLI to check whether vendor-side optimizations increase exploitation capabilities.
  Our results show a sharp capability split between publicly deployed frontier models and the private frontier. Across the 8 publicly deployed models tested, reaching the vulnerable code and triggering a crash is routine, but arbitrary code execution is not. The private model shows arbitrary code execution on approximately half. Overall, results suggest that exploit construction against hardened targets is an emerging frontier capability.

</details>


### [50] [DSTAN-Med: Dual-Channel Spatiotemporal Attention with Physiological Plausibility Filtering for False Data Injection Attack Detection in IoT-Based Medical Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14165)
*Md Mehedi Hasan,Rafiqul Islam,Md Zakir Hossain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出DSTAN-Med框架，通过双通道自注意力机制（独立处理传感器间空间关联和时间依赖）和生理合理性滤波器（PPF）检测IoMT传感器流中的虚假数据注入攻击，在三个数据集上相比最强Transformer基线TranAD灵敏度提升7.4-8.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoMT传感器流中的FDI攻击会篡改生命体征信号，威胁患者安全；现有深度学习方法将传感器间空间相关性与时间依赖性混淆在共享隐空间中，无法解耦FDI攻击同时留下的独特空间和时间特征，且缺乏利用领域知识约束输出的机制。

Method: 提出DSTAN-Med框架，包含三部分：(1) 双通道注意力机制(DAM)，通过独立的传感器级自注意力(SWA)和时间级自注意力(TWA)在正交张量轴上处理多变量传感器窗口；(2) 残差1D-CNN模块提取局部时间特征；(3) 零参数生理合理性滤波器(PPF)，抑制违反领域知识边界的攻击特征。

Result: 在PhysioNet/CinC 2012、MIMIC-III Waveform和WESAD三个数据集上，DSTAN-Med相比最强Transformer基线TranAD灵敏度提升7.4-8.3个百分点（p<0.01, McNemar检验+Holm-Bonferroni校正）；PPF独立贡献3.1-4.2个百分点的精度提升且灵敏度损失可忽略；残差连接移除导致灵敏度下降14.0个百分点。

Conclusion: DSTAN-Med通过分离空间和时间注意力路径及引入领域知识约束，有效提升了IoMT传感器流中FDI攻击的检测性能，各组件均被消融实验证明不可或缺。

Abstract: False data injection (FDI) attacks on Internet of Medical Things (IoMT) sensor streams falsify vital signs in transit, threatening patient safety and defeating clinical monitoring systems that lack cyber-physical anomaly detection capability. Existing deep learning detectors conflate inter-sensor spatial correlations with temporal dependencies in a shared latent space, preventing disentanglement of the distinct spatial and temporal signatures that FDI attacks imprint simultaneously; no current method exploits domain knowledge to constrain outputs against physiologically impossible attack patterns. We propose DSTAN-Med, a supervised framework comprising a Dual-channel Attention Mechanism (DAM) that routes multivariate sensor windows through independent sensor-wise (SWA) and time-wise (TWA) self-attention pathways operating on orthogonal tensor axes, a residual 1D-CNN block for local temporal feature extraction, and a zero-parameter Physiological Plausibility Filter (PPF) that suppresses attack signatures violating domain-knowledge bounds. Evaluated across three IoMT sensor datasets - PhysioNet/CinC 2012 (ICU vital signs), MIMIC-III Waveform (continuous ICU waveforms), and WESAD (wearable biosensor signals) - DSTAN-Med achieves mean sensitivity gains of 7.4-8.3 percentage points over the strongest Transformer baseline (TranAD), with improvements significant at p < 0.01 (McNemar's test, Holm-Bonferroni correction). The PPF contributes independent precision gains of 3.1-4.2 percentage points at negligible sensitivity cost across all three corpora. Ablation studies confirm that each component is individually necessary; removal of residual connections alone reduces sensitivity by 14.0 percentage points. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/mehedi93hasan/DSTAN-MED.

</details>


### [51] [Web Agents Should Adopt the Plan-Then-Execute Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14290)
*Julien Piet,Annabella Chow,Yiwei Hou,Muxi Lyu,Sylvie Venuto,Jinhao Zhu,Raluca Ada Popa,David Wagner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文质疑ReAct作为Web智能体默认架构的合理性，提出应默认采用"先规划后执行"(plan-then-execute)范式：在观察运行时Web内容之前，先承诺一个任务特定的程序，然后执行它。理由是ReAct将不可信的Web内容直接输入模型决策，为提示注入提供了攻击路径；而plan-then-execute改变了这一边界，不可信数据不能重定义用户任务或合成新动作。分析WebArena发现所有任务兼容该范式，80%可纯编程完成。主要障碍是Web工具（click, type, scroll）的页面依赖语义使得规划时信息不足，需要构建类型化的接口将交互从点击按键提升到任务级操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReAct已成为LLM智能体的默认架构，但其将Web上多方混合的不可信内容（如商品页面的卖家信息、用户评论、广告）直接输入模型进行动作决策，为提示注入创造了直接路径，可以劫持智能体的控制流。作者认为这在Web场景下是错误的默认选择。

Method: 1. 分析ReAct架构在Web场景下的安全缺陷（提示注入路径）；2. 提出plan-then-execute范式并定义其边界属性；3. 在WebArena基准上评估任务兼容性；4. 识别实现该范式的主要障碍并指出解决方案方向。

Result: WebArena中所有任务都兼容plan-then-execute范式，其中80%可仅通过编程化计划完成（无需运行时LLM子程序）。当前主要障碍是浏览器工具（click, type, scroll）具有页面依赖的语义，导致规划时信息不足，需要类型化接口将交互提升到任务级操作。

Conclusion: Web任务默认不需要像ReAct这样的反应式架构，而是需要类型化、完整、可审计的网站API。这是一个基础设施问题，而非建模问题。Web接口应暴露出语义清晰的操作，使智能体在执行前拥有足够信息进行规划。

Abstract: ReAct has become the default architecture across LLM agents, and many existing web agents follow this paradigm. We argue that it is the wrong default for web agents. Instead, web agents should default to plan-then-execute: commit to a task-specific program before observing runtime web content, then execute it. The reason is that web content mixes inputs from many parties. An e-commerce product page may combine a seller's listing, customer reviews and sponsored advertisements. Under ReAct, all of this content flows into the model when deciding on the next action, creating a direct path for prompt injections to steer the agent's control flow. Plan-then-execute changes this boundary: untrusted data may influence values or branches inside a predefined execution graph, but it cannot redefine the user task or cause the model to synthesize new actions at runtime. We analyze WebArena, a popular web agent benchmark, and find that all tasks are compatible with plan-then-execute, while 80% can be completed with a purely programmatic plan, without any runtime LLM subroutine. We identify the main barrier to adopting plan-then-execute on the web: For it to work well, tools must map cleanly to semantic actions, with effects known before execution, so agents have enough information to plan. The web does not naturally expose that interface. Browser tools such as click, type, and scroll have page-dependent meanings. Planning at this layer is near-sighted: the agent can only see actions on the current page, and later actions appear only after it acts. Closing this gap requires typed interfaces that turn website interactions from clicks and keystrokes to task-level operations. This is an infrastructure problem, not a modeling problem. Web tasks do not need reactivity by default; they need typed, complete, auditable website APIs.

</details>


### [52] [To See is Not to Learn: Protecting Multimodal Data from Unauthorized Fine-Tuning of Large Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14291)
*Chengshuai Zhao,Zhen Tan,Dawei Li,Zhiyuan Yu,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MMGuard是一种针对大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）的主动防御方法，通过对多模态数据注入人眼不可察觉的扰动，生成"不可学习样本"，从而防止未经授权的LVLM微调。该方法通过最小化训练损失创建优化捷径，使模型过拟合噪声；同时引入跨模态绑定干扰机制，强制噪声与训练目标之间产生虚假关联，并结合集成学习策略实现跨模型迁移。实验证明在白盒、灰盒和黑盒威胁模型下均能提供有效、隐蔽且鲁棒的防护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法（如机器遗忘、水印）均为事后补救措施，只能在知识产权侵权发生后采取行动。数据所有者缺乏一种主动保护多模态数据免受未经授权LVLM微调的有效手段。

Method: 1) 生成不可学习样本：注入人眼不可察觉的扰动，通过最小化训练损失创建优化捷径，使模型过拟合噪声，导致推理时无扰动情况下性能下降。2) 跨模态绑定干扰：策略性地偏移LVLM注意力，强制噪声与训练目标之间产生虚假关联，提供理论保证。3) 集成学习策略：增强跨模型迁移能力，使扰动在不同LVLM架构间有效。

Result: 在六个数据集上对九个开源LVLM进行了全面评估，证明MMGuard在白盒、灰盒和黑盒威胁模型下均能提供有效、隐蔽且鲁棒的防护效果。

Conclusion: MMGuard建立了机械性优势，能够主动防御针对LVLM的激进微调攻击，为多模态数据版权和隐私保护提供了一种有效的前置防护方案。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) is increasingly accompanied by unauthorized scraping and training on multimodal web data, posing severe copyright and privacy risks to data owners. Existing countermeasures, such as machine unlearning and watermarks, are inherent post-hoc approaches that act only after intellectual property infringement has already occurred. In this work, we propose MMGuard to empower data owners to proactively protect their multimodal data against unauthorized LVLM fine-tuning. MMGuard generates unlearnable examples by injecting human-imperceptible perturbations that actively exploit the learning dynamics of LVLMs. By minimizing the training loss, the perturbation creates an optimization shortcut, causing the model to overfit to the noise and thereby degrading downstream performance when the perturbation is absent during inference. To further strengthen this defense, MMGuard introduces a cross-modal binding disruption, strategically shifting LVLM attention to enforce a spurious correlation between the noise and the training target with theoretical guarantees. Enhanced by an ensemble learning strategy for cross-model transferability, MMGuard is evaluated against nine open-source LVLMs across six datasets. Our comprehensive results demonstrate effective, stealthy, and robust protection under white-box, gray-box, and black-box threat models, establishing a mechanistic advantage in proactively defending against aggressive fine-tuning exploitation.

</details>


### [53] [The Great Pretender: A Stochasticity Problem in LLM Jailbreak](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14418)
*Jean-Philippe Monteuuis,Cong Chen,Jonathan Petit*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示当前越狱攻击研究中的ASR指标不稳定且被系统性地夸大，并提出新的评估框架CAS-eval和攻击生成框架CAS-gen，通过考虑随机性影响，将攻击在一轮尝试中30%的成功率损失恢复回来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到越狱攻击的成功率（ASR）存在不可靠性：同一个提示在多次尝试中表现不一致，且论文中报告的ASR数值被系统性地夸大，导致不同论文之间无法公平比较。

Method: 系统研究随机性在攻击评估和攻击生成两个阶段的影响；提出新指标和新框架：CAS-eval（一致性感知评估框架）和CAS-gen（一致性感知攻击生成框架）。

Result: CAS-eval发现，当要求越狱提示在多次尝试中均成功时，ASR会下降多达30个百分点；CAS-gen能够恢复这30个百分点的损失。

Conclusion: ASR作为主要指标不稳定且不可信，需引入一致性维度；CAS框架能有效弥补现有方法缺陷，提升越狱攻击的实际可靠性。

Abstract: "Oh-Oh, yes, I'm the great pretender. Pretending that I'm doing well. My need is such, I pretend too much..." summarizes the state in the area of jailbreak creation and evaluation. You find this method to generate adversarial attacks proposed by a reputable institution (e.g., BoN from Anthropic or Crescendo from Microsoft Research). However, this method does not deliver on the promise claimed in the paper despite having top ASR scores against industry-grade LLMs. You successfully generate the jailbreak prompts against your target (open) model. However, the generated jailbreak prompt works against the target model with a 50% consecutive success rate (5 out of 10 attempts) despite having an 80% ASR (on paper) on the latest closed-source model (with a guardrail system)! This observation leads us to think. First, Attack Success Rate (ASR), the primary metric for LLM jailbreak benchmarking, is not a stable quantity. Second, published ASR numbers are therefore systematically inflated and incomparable across papers. Therefore, we wonder "Why a successful jailbreak prompt does not perform consistently well against a target model on which the prompts have been optimized?". To answer this question, we study the impact of stochasticity not only during attack evaluation but also during attack generation. Our evaluation includes several jailbreak attacks, models (different sizes and providers), and judges. In addition, we propose a new metric and two new frameworks (CAS-eval and CAS-gen). Our evaluation framework, CAS-eval, shows that an attack can have an ASR drop of up to 30 percentage points when a jailbreak prompt needs to succeed on more than one attempt. Thankfully, our attack generation framework (CAS-gen) improves previous jailbreak methods and helps them recover this loss of 30 percentage points!

</details>


### [54] [MemLineage: Lineage-Guided Enforcement for LLM Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14421)
*Ciyan Ouyang,Rui Hou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MemLineage 是一种针对 LLM 智能体记忆的防御机制，通过密码学溯源（Ed25519 签名 + RFC-6962 Merkle 日志）和 LLM 中介的派生谱系（加权推导 DAG），将敏感操作门控限制为仅允许源自可信祖先的决策，在不干扰良性召回的前提下，将三种记忆投毒工作负载的攻击成功率降至零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明，不可信内容可被写入持久化智能体状态，并在后续会话中作为指令重新进入，导致记忆中毒攻击。现有方法将问题视为过滤问题，但作者认为这本质上是监管链（chain-of-custody）问题，需要在保持有用记忆召回的同时，防止不可信状态证明敏感操作。

Method: 设计了一个六模块系统：1）基于 RFC-6962 Merkle 日志的不可变记录；2）每个主体使用 Ed25519 签名；3）加权推导 DAG 记录检索条目对新记忆的影响；4）最大强边传播规则，确保不可信路径持久性；5）敏感操作门控，拒绝源自外部祖先的主动理由的调度；6）确定性机制隔离测试框架。

Result: 在确定性机制隔离框架中，面对三种记忆投毒负载，MemLineage 是唯一将所有攻击成功率降至零的配置，每次操作开销低于毫秒级。在 AgentDojo 桥接测试中，无防御和仅签名基线在所有六组银行操作对上均失败，而所有 MemLineage 行将严格 AgentDojo ASR 降至零。

Conclusion: 将智能体记忆安全问题从过滤问题重新定义为监管链问题，通过密码学溯源与谱系追踪的组合，能够在毫秒级开销下彻底消除记忆中毒攻击，且不影响良性记忆召回能力。

Abstract: We introduce MemLineage, a defense for LLM agent memory that attaches both cryptographic provenance and LLM-mediated derivation lineage to every entry. Recent and concurrent work shows that untrusted content can be written into persistent agent state and re-enter later sessions as an instruction; the remaining systems question is how to preserve useful memory recall while preventing such state from justifying sensitive actions. MemLineage treats this as a chain-of-custody problem rather than a filtering problem. It is a six-module design around an RFC-6962 Merkle log over per-principal Ed25519-signed entries: a weighted derivation DAG records which retrieved entries influenced each new memory, and a max-of-strong-edges propagation rule makes Untrusted-Path Persistence hold for any chain whose attribution edges remain above threshold. The sensitive-action gate then refuses dispatches whose active justification descends from an external ancestor, while still allowing benign recall. We evaluate three defense cells against three memory-poisoning workloads on a deterministic mechanism-isolation harness; MemLineage is the only configuration in that harness that drives all three columns to zero ASR, while sub-millisecond per-operation overhead keeps it well below the noise floor of any LLM call. A Codex-backed AgentDojo bridge further separates strong-model behavior from defense-layer behavior: under an intentionally vulnerable tool-output profile, no-defense and signature-only baselines fail on all six banking pairs, while all MemLineage rows reduce strict AgentDojo ASR to zero. The core deterministic artifacts are byte-equal CI-verified; hosted-model AgentDojo and live-model sweeps are recorded as auditable logs rather than byte-pinned artifacts.

</details>


### [55] [Exploiting LLM Agent Supply Chains via Payload-less Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14460)
*Xinyu Liu,Yukai Zhao,Xing Hu,Xin Xia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对LLM驱动自主智能体的无载荷供应链攻击——语义合规劫持（SCH），通过将恶意目标转化为看似合规的自然语言指令，诱使智能体在运行时动态生成并执行未授权代码。实验表明，在最脆弱配置下，SCH对机密性破坏和远程代码执行的成功率分别高达77.67%和67.33%，且由于不包含可识别的AST签名和显式恶意意图，恶意技能文件检测率为0.00%。研究揭示了从签名检测向语义意图验证转变的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有审计机制能有效识别显式代码载荷和预定义威胁内容，但当恶意行为缺乏直接注入、而是通过智能体自身的生成能力在运行时动态合成时，这些检测机制就会被绕过。作者旨在探索这一盲区，揭示智能体供应链中未被充分研究的攻击面。

Method: SCH攻击将恶意目标转化为非结构化的自然语言指令，格式化为必要的合规规则，从而诱导智能体生成并执行未授权代码。作者开发了自动化评估管线，在三个主流智能体框架和三个基础模型的测试矩阵中结合情境化场景进行测试，并引入多技能自动优化（MS-AO）进一步提升攻击效果。

Result: 在最脆弱配置下，SCH在机密性破坏和远程代码执行（RCE）上分别达到77.67%和67.33%的成功率；MS-AO进一步提升了攻击效能。由于省略了可识别的抽象语法树（AST）签名和显式恶意意图，恶意技能文件的检测率为0.00%，成功规避了现有扫描工具。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了智能体供应链中一个未被充分探索的攻击面，指出安全机制需要从基于签名的检测模型向语义意图验证转变。

Abstract: Autonomous agents powered by Large Language Models (LLMs) acquire external functionalities through third-party skills available in open marketplaces. Adopting these integrations broadens the potential attack surface, prompting a need for systematic security evaluation. Current auditing mechanisms are effective at identifying explicit code payloads and predefined threat contents through security scanning. These detection mechanisms are bypassed if malicious behaviors lack direct injection and are instead synthesized dynamically at runtime through the agent's inherent generative capabilities. Exploring this blind spot, we introduce Semantic Compliance Hijacking (SCH), a payload-less supply chain attack targeting autonomous coding environments. The SCH approach translates malicious goals into unstructured natural language instructions formatted as necessary compliance rules, leading the agent to generate and execute unauthorized code. To assess the real-world viability of this attack, we developed an automated pipeline to evaluate its effectiveness across a test matrix comprising three mainstream agent frameworks and three distinct foundation models using contextualized scenarios. The findings demonstrate the pervasive nature of this threat, with SCH achieving peak success rates of up to 77.67% for confidentiality breaches and 67.33% for Remote Code Execution (RCE) under the most vulnerable configurations. Furthermore, the introduction of Multi-Skill Automated Optimization (MS-AO) further boosted attack efficacy. By omitting recognizable Abstract Syntax Tree (AST) signatures and explicit harmful intents, the manipulated skill files maintained a 0.00% detection rate, evading current scanning tools. This research highlights an underexplored attack surface within agent supply chains, pointing to a necessary transition from signature-based detection models toward semantic intent validation.

</details>


### [56] [Defenses at Odds: Measuring and Explaining Defense Conflicts in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14514)
*Xiangtao Meng,Wenyu Chen,Chuanchao Zang,Xinyu Gao,Jianing Wang,Li Wang,Zheng Li,Shanqing Guo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 首次系统研究了LLM在序列部署中跨防御交互问题，发现38.9%的防御序列会导致原始风险加剧，并通过机制分析揭示层间参数冲突是主因，提出冲突引导的层冻结策略作为缓解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御手段几乎都在一次性部署假设下单独评估，但实际中LLM是增量式打补丁部署的，后续防御是否会破坏先前防御的保护效果尚不清楚。

Method: 评估144种有序序列（3种风险维度×3种模型家族），采用层间表征散度、激活修补、PCA轨迹分析进行机制研究，提出层间冲突分数量化几何张力，并设计冲突引导的层冻结缓解方法。

Result: 38.9%的序列出现原始维度的风险加剧；冲突序列中关键层参数更新呈强反对齐，良性序列则保持近正交；防御崩溃源于共享层激活模式反转。

Conclusion: 序列部署中跨防御交互普遍存在且高度不对称、顺序依赖；通过层冻结高冲突层可在不损害后续防御性能的前提下保留先前保护。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) deployed in high-stakes applications must simultaneously manage multiple risks, yet existing defenses are almost exclusively evaluated in isolation under a one-shot deployment assumption. In practice, providers patch models incrementally throughout their lifecycle-responding to newly exposed vulnerabilities or targeted data-removal requests without retraining from scratch. This raises a fundamental but underexplored question: does a later defense preserve the protections established by an earlier one? We present the first systematic study of cross-defense interactions under sequential deployment. Evaluating 144 ordered sequences across three risk dimensions and three model families, we find that 38.9% exhibit measurable risk exacerbation on the originally defended dimension. These interactions are highly asymmetric and order-dependent. To explain these phenomena, we conduct a mechanistic analysis on representative deployment sequences. Using layer-wise representational divergence and activation patching, we localize each defense to a compact set of critical layers. In conflicting sequences, the overlapping critical layers exhibit strongly anti-aligned parameter updates, whereas benign orderings maintain near-orthogonal updates. PCA trajectory analysis reveals that defense collapse stems from activation pattern reversals in these shared layers. We further introduce a layer-wise conflict score that quantifies the geometric tension between defense-induced activation subspaces, offering mechanistic insight into the observed reversals. Guided by this diagnosis, we propose conflict-guided layer freezing, a lightweight mitigation that selectively freezes high-conflict layers during sequential deployment, preserving prior protections without degrading secondary defense performance.

</details>


### [57] [Privacy Auditing with Zero (0) Training Run](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14591)
*Tudor Cebere,Mathieu Even,Linus Bleistein,Aurélien Bellet*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Zero-Run隐私审计方法，在无法重新训练或控制数据插入的场景下，利用两个固定数据集（已知成员和非成员）对模型进行事后差分隐私审计，并通过因果推断方法校正成员与非成员分布差异带来的混杂偏差，实现有效的隐私下界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私审计方法需要对训练管线进行干预性访问（多次重新训练或随机化数据包含），这对大型已部署系统（如基础模型）往往不可行。因此需要一种事后（post-hoc）审计框架，仅使用固定数据集即可进行有效隐私评估。

Method: 提出Zero-Run审计框架：1) 将分布偏移与算法泄露的联合效应建模为自适应组合，产生保守的全局校正；2) 基于观测数据条件化并调整逐点成员关系猜测，产生更尖锐的实例依赖界限。两种方法均借鉴因果推断思想对混杂效应进行形式化校正。

Result: 在合成数据和大规模模型上的实验表明，Zero-Run审计能够在无法重新训练或受控数据插入的情况下实现实用的隐私评估。

Conclusion: Zero-Run审计为事后场景提供了可行的差分隐私下界估计方法，解决了现有方法对干预性访问的依赖问题。

Abstract: Privacy auditing provides empirical lower bounds on the differential privacy parameters of learning algorithms. Existing methods, however, require interventional access to the training pipeline, either to retrain multiple times or to randomize data inclusion. This is often infeasible for large deployed systems such as foundation models. We introduce Zero-Run privacy auditing, a post-hoc framework for auditing models using two fixed datasets: examples known to be training-set members and examples known to be non-members. In this observational regime, membership is no longer randomized; instead, member and non-member data often differ in distribution, so membership inference scores may reflect a distribution shift rather than algorithmic leakage. Drawing on ideas from causal inference, we formalize this confounding effect and propose two complementary corrections that yield valid privacy audits. Our first approach models the combined effect of distribution shift and algorithmic leakage as an adaptive composition, producing conservative global corrections. Our second approach conditions on observed data and adjusts pointwise membership guesses, yielding sharper instance-dependent bounds. Experiments on synthetic data and large-scale models show that Zero-Run auditing enables practical privacy evaluation when retraining or controlled data insertion is infeasible.

</details>


### [58] [One Step to the Side: Why Defenses Against Malicious Finetuning Fail Under Adaptive Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14605)
*Itay Zloczower,Eyal Lenga,Gilad Gressel,Yisroel Mirsky*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 当前针对大模型微调攻击的防御措施存在根本性缺陷：它们只是隐藏而非消除模型的有害行为。本文提出了统一自适应攻击方法，能够突破15种现有防御机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型提供商通过开源权重或API允许用户微调基础模型，但现有的安全对齐在微调过程中容易被有害数据破坏。虽然已有多种防御方案被提出，但这些防御仅针对固定攻击进行评估，没有考虑攻击方可能自适应调整策略。

Method: 首先系统梳理了15种近期防御方案，识别出它们的共同缺陷：仅遮蔽或误导通往有害行为的路径，而非真正移除有害行为。然后设计了一种统一的自适应攻击方法，能够针对所有防御机制进行有效攻击。

Result: 统一自适应攻击成功突破了所有15种防御机制，证明现有防御方法无法提供稳健的安全性，它们只能防御设计时针对的特定攻击。

Conclusion: 当前防御方法无法提供真正鲁棒的安全保障，希望提出的统一自适应攻击方法能帮助未来研究者和实践者在部署防御前进行有效的压力测试。

Abstract: Model providers increasingly release open weights or allow users to fine-tune foundation models through APIs. Although these models are safety-aligned before release, their safeguards can often be removed by fine-tuning on harmful data. Recent defenses aim to make models robust to such malicious fine-tuning, but they are largely evaluated only against fixed attacks that do not account for the defense. We show that these robustness claims are incomplete. Surveying 15 recent defenses, we identify several defense mechanisms and show that they share a single weakness: they obscure or misdirect the path to harmful behavior without removing the behavior itself. We then develop a unified adaptive attack that breaks defenses across all defense mechanisms. Our results show that current approaches do not provide robust security; they mainly stop the attacks they were designed against. We hope that our unified adaptive adversary for this domain will help future researchers and practitioners stress-test new defenses before deployment.

</details>


### [59] [Capacitive Touchscreens at Risk: A Practical Side-Channel Attack on Smartphones via Electromagnetic Emanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14633)
*Yukun Cheng,Changhai Ou,Shiyu Zhu,Jinyuan Zhang,Zhenfang Qiu,Xingshuo Han,Tianwei Zhang,Yuan Li,Shihui Zheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TESLA是一种新型非接触式电磁侧信道攻击，通过窃听触摸屏扫描时的电磁辐射，能够以极高准确率恢复PIN码（99.3%）、键盘输入（97.6%）、应用类别（95.0%）及手写轨迹（几何相似度0.74），在真实场景下攻击范围更广、样本获取更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电容触屏侧信道攻击往往需要限制性条件或侵入式测量，缺乏一种同时具备非接触性、广泛攻击目标且适用于实际场景的统一攻击方法。

Method: 利用触摸屏扫描过程中固有的电磁辐射，将其作为统一的信息泄漏源，通过秘密放置近场电磁探头捕获触控交互的时空演化信号，进而重建用户输入信息。

Result: 在iPhone X、小米10 Pro、三星S10、华为Mate 30 Pro上实现PIN码识别99.3%、键盘输入重建97.6%、应用推断95.0%、字符识别76.8%、2D手写轨迹Jaccard指数0.74。

Conclusion: 电磁侧信道攻击在触摸屏场景下具有统一泄漏基础和广泛实用性，对现代智能手机安全构成严重威胁。

Abstract: Capacitive touchscreens in modern smartphones introduce severe side-channel vulnerabilities. However, existing attacks often require restrictive conditions or invasive measurements. This paper presents TESLA, a novel, contactless electromagnetic (EM) side-channel attack that exploits inherent EM emanations during touchscreen scanning. We demonstrate that these emanations encode the spatiotemporal evolution of touch interactions, forming a unified leakage basis. By secretly placing an EM probe near the victim's device, TESLA enables attackers to extract highly sensitive information, including screen-unlocking PIN codes, keyboard inputs, interacting application categories, and continuous handwriting trajectories. Compared to existing attacks, TESLA offers a broader range of attack targets, more efficient sample acquisition, and operations in practical attack scenarios. Extensive evaluations on popular commercial smartphones, specifically the iPhone X, Xiaomi 10 Pro, Samsung S10, and Huawei Mate 30 Pro, validate the effectiveness of TESLA. It achieves remarkable inference accuracy in diverse settings such as private meeting rooms and public libraries, with success rates of 99.3% for PIN code recognition, 97.6% for keyboard input reconstruction, and 95.0% for application inference, respectively. Simultaneously, it attains a 76.8% character recognition accuracy and a high geometric similarity (Jaccard index of 0.74) for 2D handwriting trajectory reconstruction.

</details>


### [60] [Adapting AlphaEvolve to Optimize Fully Homomorphic Encryption on TPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14718)
*Shruthi Gorantala,Jianming Tong,Asra Ali,Baiyu Li,Jonathan Katz,Jeremy Kun,Thomas Steinke,Abhradeep Thakurta,Julian Walker,Amir Yazdanbakhsh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AlphaEvolve，利用LLM驱动的进化搜索自动探索FHE硬件感知优化，在TPUv5e上实现TFHE bootstrap 2.5倍加速和CKKS rotation/multiplication 1.31/1.18倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FHE部署受限于高计算开销，现有专用硬件加速器（如TPU）上的密码学内核映射困难，编译器栈抽象底层硬件利用，开发依赖手动试错，导致碎片化执行和资源利用不足。

Method: 将优化建模为进化搜索问题，利用AlphaEvolve闭环系统结合LLM驱动的代码生成，通过硬件执行真实反馈和正确性测试指导进化过程，自动探索硬件感知的密码学内核优化。

Result: 在Google Cloud TPUv5e上，24小时内自动探索发现：TFHE bootstrap延迟提升2.5倍，CKKS rotation和multiplication延迟分别提升1.31倍和1.18倍。

Conclusion: AlphaEvolve能够帮助研究人员在密码学、编译器和硬件加速器之间导航优化权衡，自动化FHE硬件优化流程。

Abstract: The deployment of Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) at scale is hindered due to its heavy computational overhead. While specialized hardware accelerators like Google Tensor Processing Units (TPUs) can help, mapping complex cryptographic kernels onto such architectures remains a challenge. Efficient execution requires co-optimization between the systolic array-based Matrix Multiplication Unit (MXU) and Vector Processing Units (VPUs), as well as the orchestration of data movement across the vector register files. Existing compiler stacks often abstract low-level hardware utilization, requiring developers to adopt a manual trial-and-error process that often results in fragmented execution and underutilized resources. To accelerate this development process, we use AlphaEvolve to automate the exploration of hardware-aware cryptographic-kernel optimizations. We frame optimization as an evolutionary search problem, utilizing the closed-loop system provided by AlphaEvolve, that leverages LLM-driven code generation. We use real-world feedback from hardware execution and rigorous correctness testing to guide the evolution process. We evaluate AlphaEvolve optimization on primitives for both the TFHE (Jaxite) and CKKS (CROSS) FHE schemes on Google Cloud TPUv5e, a contemporary TPU architecture. Within 24 hours of automated exploration, AlphaEvolve discovered implementation-level optimizations that improve TFHE bootstrap latency by 2.5x and CKKS rotation and multiplication latency by 1.31x and 1.18x, respectively, relative to human-engineered state of the art. These results demonstrate that AlphaEvolve can be used to enable researchers to navigate the optimization trade-offs between cryptography, compilers, and hardware accelerators.

</details>


### [61] [EVA: Editing for Versatile Alignment against Jailbreaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14750)
*Yi Wang,Hongye Qiu,Yue Xu,Sibei Yang,Zhan Qin,Minlie Huang,Wenjie Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: EVA提出了一种基于直接模型编辑的安全对齐框架，通过精确识别并修改导致模型易受有害指令影响的特定神经元，在不影响通用推理能力的前提下有效防御越狱攻击，适用于LLM和VLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法（如安全微调或外部过滤器）在减少有害内容生成方面有一定效果，但存在计算开销大、安全性与实用性之间存在权衡（牺牲良性任务性能）等问题，亟需一种精确且高效的解决方案。

Method: EVA将安全对齐重构为精确的知识修正任务，通过定位并外科手术式地编辑模型中与有害指令敏感性相关的特定神经元，而非重新训练大量参数，实现局部更新以中和有害行为。

Result: 大量实验表明，EVA在缓解LLM和VLM的越狱攻击方面优于基线方法，提供了一种精确且高效的后部署安全对齐方案。

Conclusion: EVA通过直接模型编辑有效解决了安全对齐中的效率与性能权衡问题，为模型后部署阶段的安全维护提供了一种新的精确范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities but remain vulnerable to jailbreaking attacks, where adversaries exploit textual or visual triggers to bypass safety guardrails. Recent defenses typically rely on safety fine-tuning or external filters to reduce the model's likelihood of producing harmful content. While effective to some extent, these methods often incur significant computational overheads and suffer from the safety utility trade-off, degrading the model's performance on benign tasks. To address these challenges, we propose EVA (Editing for Versatile Alignment against Jailbreaks), a novel framework that pioneers the application of direct model editing for safety alignment. EVA reframes safety alignment as a precise knowledge correction task. Instead of retraining massive parameters, EVA identifies and surgically edits specific neurons responsible for the model's susceptibility to harmful instructions, while leaving the vast majority of the model unchanged. By localizing the updates, EVA effectively neutralizes harmful behaviors without compromising the model's general reasoning capabilities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EVA outperforms baselines in mitigating jailbreaks across both LLMs and VLMs, offering a precise and efficient solution for post-deployment safety alignment.

</details>


### [62] [Do Coding Agents Understand Least-Privilege Authorization?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14859)
*Zheng Yan,Jingxiang Weng,Charles Chen,Dengyun Peng,Ethan Qin,Jiannan Guan,Jinhao Liu,Qiming Yu,Yixin Yuan,Fanqing Meng,Carl Che,Mengkang Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出权限边界推理任务和AuthBench基准，发现当前模型在自动化授权中无法平衡充分性与最小权限，存在模型特定的授权吸引子；为此提出充分性-紧致性分解方法，通过正向模拟生成覆盖策略再逐项审计，显著提升安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体需要访问shell、仓库和用户文件，最小权限授权是其安全部署的前提条件。当前模型无法自主推断任务所需的权限边界，因此需要研究模型能否（以及如何）在给定任务指令和终端环境的情况下，推断出合理的文件级读写执行策略。

Method: 1) 提出权限边界推理任务，将问题形式化为从任务指令+终端环境到文件级策略的映射；2) 构建AuthBench基准，包含120个真实终端任务，附带人工审核的权限标签和可执行验证器；3) 提出充分性-紧致性分解方法：首先生成覆盖导向策略（正向模拟任务），然后逐项审计每个授权条目的合理性和敏感性。

Result: 前沿模型在授权中经常既遗漏执行链所需的权限，又授予未使用或敏感的访问权限。增加推理时间不会解决这种不匹配，反而使每个模型趋向其特定的授权吸引子（更一致的失败模式）。充分性-紧致性分解在紧致偏向模型上提升了最多15.8%的敏感任务成功率，同时降低了所有评估模型的攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 直接策略生成是瓶颈，因为单次生成必须同时发现所有必要访问并拒绝所有不必要访问。充分性-紧致性分解通过将任务拆分为覆盖生成和审计两个阶段，有效解决了这一矛盾，是实现安全自主授权的重要方向。

Abstract: As coding agents gain access to shells, repositories, and user files, least-privilege authorization becomes a prerequisite for safe deployment: an agent should receive enough authority to complete the task, without unnecessary authority that exposes sensitive surfaces.To study whether current models can infer this boundary themselves, we first introduce permission-boundary inference, where a model maps a task instruction and terminal environment to a file-level read/write/execute policy, and AuthBench, a benchmark of 120 realistic terminal tasks with human-reviewed permission labels and executable validators for utility and attack outcomes.AuthBench shows that authorization is not a simple conservative-versus-permissive calibration problem: frontier models often omit permissions required by the execution chain while also granting unused or sensitive accesses.Increasing inference-time reasoning does not resolve this mismatch. Instead, each model moves toward a model-specific authorization attractor: more reasoning makes it more consistent in its own failure mode, whether broad-but-exposed or tight-but-brittle.This suggests that direct policy generation is the bottleneck, because a single generation must both discover all necessary accesses and reject all unnecessary ones.We therefore propose Sufficiency-Tightness Decomposition, which first generates a coverage-oriented policy by forward-simulating the task and then audits each granted entry for grounding and sensitivity.Across tested models, this decomposition improves sensitive-task success by up to 15.8% on tightness-biased models while reducing attack success across all evaluated models.

</details>


### [63] [Toward Securing AI Agents Like Operating Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14932)
*Lukas Pirch,Micha Horlboge,Patrick Großmann,Syeda Mahnur Asif,Klim Kireev,Thorsten Holz,Konrad Rieck*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文从操作系统安全视角审视基于LLM的自主智能体安全，发现当前智能体系统在资源隔离、权限分离和通信中介方面面临与早期操作系统相似的挑战。通过对开源智能体的调研和案例分析，指出即使攻击能力有限，现有保护机制仍有缺陷，但许多漏洞可通过成熟的OS安全技术缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的自主智能体（如OpenClaw）正快速普及，其广泛工具使用和深度环境集成带来了显著安全风险。作者认为智能体安全与操作系统安全面临相似的资源隔离、权限分离和通信中介问题，因此借鉴OS视角系统性地分析和解决智能体安全挑战。

Method: 首先从OS安全视角论证智能体系统面临相似挑战；其次调研开源智能体生态，推导统一架构；然后系统性分析潜在攻击向量；最后通过案例研究评估四种OpenClaw类智能体在实际攻击下的安全表现。

Result: 即使攻击者能力有限，现有智能体保护机制在实践中仍存在多处失败；安全运行需要详细系统知识和仔细配置。部分智能体能力存在设计层面的不安全问题，但多数漏洞可通过成熟的OS安全技术（如资源隔离、权限分离）得到缓解。

Conclusion: 提出一系列智能体系统安全设计建议，强调将OS安全成熟技术应用于智能体架构设计中是实现安全自主系统的可行路径。

Abstract: Autonomous agents based on large language models (LLMs) are rapidly emerging as a general-purpose technology, with recent systems such as OpenClaw extending their capabilities through broad tool use, third-party skills, and deeper integration into user environments. At the same time, these agentic systems introduce substantial security risks by combining unconstrained capabilities with access to sensitive user data. In this work, we investigate the security of LLM-based agents through the lens of operating systems. We argue that both face strikingly similar challenges in isolating resources, separating privileges, and mediating communication.
  Guided by this perspective, we survey the current landscape of open-source agents, derive a unified agent architecture, and systematically analyze potential attack vectors. To validate this analysis, we conduct a case study evaluating four widely used OpenClaw-like agents. Even under modest attacker capabilities, we find that several protection mechanisms fail in practice and that secure operation requires detailed system knowledge and careful configuration. However, we also observe that while some agentic capabilities remain insecure by design, many vulnerabilities can be mitigated using well-established techniques from operating system security. We conclude with a set of recommendations for the secure design of agentic systems.

</details>


### [64] [WARD: Adversarially Robust Defense of Web Agents Against Prompt Injections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15030)
*Tri Cao,Yulin Chen,Hieu Cao,Yibo Li,Khoi Le,Thong Nguyen,Yuexin Li,Yufei He,Yue Liu,Shuicheng Yan,Bryan Hooi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: WARD提出了一个针对Web智能体的提示注入防御框架，包含大规模数据集WARD-Base（177K样本/719个URL）、专用攻击数据集WARD-PIG、以及自适应对抗训练框架A3T，实现了近乎完美的OOD召回率、低误报率、抗攻击鲁棒性，且无额外延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Web智能体在开放网页环境中易受嵌入HTML或视觉界面的提示注入攻击，而现有的防护模型存在泛化能力差、误报率高、部署延迟大、易受自适应攻击等问题。

Method: WARD框架包含三个核心组件：1) WARD-Base数据集（719个高流量URL/平台，约177K样本）；2) WARD-PIG专用提示注入攻击数据集；3) A3T自适应对抗训练框架，通过基于记忆的攻击器和防护器协同进化来迭代强化模型。模型可并行运行，不增加额外延迟。

Result: 在OOD基准上达到近乎完美的召回率，对良性内容保持低误报率以维护智能体实用性，在显著分布偏移下对防护目标攻击和自适应攻击均具有鲁棒性，且并行运行效率高，不引入额外延迟。

Conclusion: WARD是一种实用、高效且鲁棒的Web智能体提示注入防御方案，解决了现有工作中的泛化性、误报率、效率和安全性四大关键问题。

Abstract: Web agents can autonomously complete online tasks by interacting with websites, but their exposure to open web environments makes them vulnerable to prompt injection attacks embedded in HTML content or visual interfaces. Existing guard models still suffer from limited generalization to unseen domains and attack patterns, high false positive rates on benign content, reduced deployment efficiency due to added latency at each step, and vulnerability to adversarial attacks that evolve over time or directly target the guard itself. To address these limitations, we propose WARD (Web Agent Robust Defense against Prompt Injection), a practical guard model for secure and efficient web agents. WARD is built on WARD-Base, a large-scale dataset with around 177K samples collected from 719 high-traffic URLs and platforms, and WARD-PIG, a dedicated dataset designed for prompt injection attacks targeting the guard model. We further introduce A3T, an adaptive adversarial attack training framework that iteratively strengthens WARD through a memory-based attacker and guard co-evolution process. Extensive experiments show that WARD achieves nearly perfect recall on out-of-distribution benchmarks, maintains low false positive rates to preserve agent utility, remains robust against guard-targeted and adaptive attacks under substantial distribution shifts, and runs efficiently in parallel with the agent without introducing additional latency.

</details>


### [65] [Analyzing Codes of Conduct for Online Safety in Video Games at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15047)
*Jiuming Jiang,Shidong Pan,Daniel W Woods,Jingjie Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文开发了CONDUCTIFY流水线，对Steam平台上9,586款多人游戏中350款可获取的行为准则（CoC）进行了大规模分析，发现CoC在热门、成人向和社区驱动型游戏中更常见，多数多人游戏缺乏CoC；同时发现游戏对传统安全和违规行为的治理较为一致，但对人际伤害和未成年人安全等问题的表述缺乏特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏公司通过发布行为准则（CoC）来阐述在线安全规则并引导玩家获取安全资源，但学界对CoC的普及程度、所治理的安全隐私违规类型以及是否满足监管和行业期望尚不清楚。

Method: 开发了CONDUCTIFY流水线，用于大规模识别和分析行为准则（CoC），并将其应用于最大的PC游戏平台Steam上的9,586款多人游戏。

Result: 在Steam的9,586款多人游戏中，仅有350款游戏可获得CoC；CoC在热门、成人向和社区驱动型游戏中更易获得；超过80%有CoC的游戏一致地处理传统安全和违规行为，但对不同类型违规的治理方式存在显著差异；与游戏机制相关的伤害相比，人际伤害和未成年人安全的表述特异性较低。

Conclusion: 研究结果有助于改进在线安全治理和CoC执行实践，为玩家和开发者社区构建更好的安全基础设施。

Abstract: Online video games have become major online social spaces where users interact, compete, and create together. These spaces, however, expose users to a wide spectrum of online harms, including harassment, discrimination, inappropriate content, privacy breach, cheating, and more. The shape and severity of such harms vary across game design, mechanics, and community context. To mitigate these harms, game companies issue Codes of Conduct (CoCs) that articulate online safety rules and direct players to safety resources. However, it remains unclear how prevalent CoCs are, what safety, security and privacy violations they govern, and whether they meet growing regulatory and industry expectations. We develop and leverage CONDUCTIFY, a pipeline for identifying and analyzing CoCs at scale. Applied to Steam, the largest PC game marketplace, it located the available CoCs for 350 of the 9,586 multiplayer titles on Steam. We found that CoCs are more available among popular, adult-oriented, and community-driven games, while most multiplayer games operate without CoCs despite regulatory and industry recommendations. Although over 80% of the games with CoCs available consistently address traditional security and safety violations, their governance approaches vary substantially across types of violations. A further asymmetry emerges in specificity. Compared with harms related to gameplay mechanics, the articulations of interpersonal harm and the underage player safety are often less specific, despite their relevance to many game communities. Together, these results inform the improvement of online safety governance and CoC enforcement practices, and building better safety infrastructure for the community of players and developers.

</details>


### [66] [PickleFuzzer: A Case Study in Fuzzing for Discrepancies Between Python Pickle Implementations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15084)
*Justin Applegate,Andreas Kellas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PickleFuzzer，一种基于生成式模糊测试的方法，用于检测Python pickle序列化协议在不同实现之间的行为差异（不一致性），发现了14个新差异，其中4个可绕过Hugging Face等平台的安全扫描工具，并获得$750漏洞赏金。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python的pickle协议虽不安全但被广泛使用（尤其机器学习模型保存），现有防御手段（限制导入、静态/动态分析）依赖于对Pickle虚拟机（PVM）操作码的准确解释，而Python三个原生PVM模块之间存在不一致性，可能导致恶意载荷被错误检测，削弱现有防御。

Method: 提出PickleFuzzer，一种基于生成式模糊测试的方法。它通过定义语法生成pickle对象，将其传递给不同的pickle实现，比较各实现运行时的异常抛出现象或关键内部状态变化来检测差异，无需规范定义的标准答案（oracle）。

Result: 检测到14个新差异，其中4个为关键差异，可绕过Hugging Face等平台的安全扫描工具；已向Python软件基金会报告，并通过漏洞赏金平台获得$750奖励。

Conclusion: 差分测试是识别pickle实现中安全相关不一致性的有效方法，未来可通过更定向的模糊测试发现更深层的pickle漏洞。

Abstract: Python's native serialization protocol, pickle, is a powerful but insecure format for transferring untrusted data. It is frequently used, especially for saving machine learning models, despite known security challenges. While developers sometimes mitigate this risk by restricting imports during unpickling or using static and dynamic analysis tools, these approaches are error-prone and depend heavily on accurate interpretations of the Pickle Virtual Machine (PVM) opcodes. Discrepancies across Python's three native PVM modules can lead to incorrect detection of malicious payloads and undermine existing defenses.
  To efficiently and scalably identify discrepancies, we present PickleFuzzer, a custom generation-based fuzzer that identifies inconsistencies across pickle implementations. PickleFuzzer generates pickle objects, passes them to each implementation, and detects differences in thrown exceptions or changes to key internal states. It generates pickle objects using a grammar, which we developed to account for the missing pickle specification. It determines discrepancies by comparing the execution behaviors of each test implementation, rather than requiring a specification-derived oracle. PickleFuzzer detected 14 new discrepancies between the pickle implementations. Four discrepancies are critical and can be used to bypass security-critical scanning tools like those deployed on the popular model hosting platform, Hugging Face. We disclosed all findings to the Python Software Foundation for remediation, and additionally disclosed the security issues to a bug bounty platform and were awarded a $750 bounty. We demonstrate that differential testing is a viable approach for identifying security-relevant discrepancies in important pickle implementations, and our work can lead to promising future directions for finding deeper pickle bugs with more directed fuzzing.

</details>


### [67] [Talk is (Not) Cheap: A Taxonomy and Benchmark Coverage Audit for LLM Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15118)
*Karthik Raghu Iyer,Yazdan Jamshidi,Nicholas Bray,Alexey A. Shvets*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于STRIDE的4×6目标×技术矩阵，用于审计LLM攻击基准的威胁覆盖面。从932篇安全研究中提取507叶分类法，发现六个主流基准仅覆盖矩阵最多25%的单元格，且存在命名碎片化与评估空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM攻击基准缺乏统一的威胁覆盖面评估框架，无法判断基准集是否全面覆盖了真实的攻击面，存在评估盲区。

Method: 构建4×6目标×技术矩阵（基于STRIDE），从932篇arXiv安全研究（2023-2026）中提取507叶分类法（401个数据填充+106个威胁模型推导），分析六个公开基准的覆盖情况。

Result: 三大主要基准（HarmBench、InjecAgent、AgentDojo）占据不重叠的单元格，最多仅覆盖矩阵的25%；服务中断和模型内部等STRIDE类别缺乏标准化评估，尽管已有攻击实现46倍令牌放大和96%成功率；2,521个攻击组存在命名碎片化（单一攻击最多29种表述形式）且高度集中于安全与对齐绕过。

Conclusion: 当前LLM攻击基准对威胁面的覆盖严重不足，存在系统性评估空白；提出的矩阵可作为可扩展工件供后续基准映射，以追踪评估缺口是否被填补。

Abstract: We introduce a reusable framework for auditing whether LLM attack benchmarks collectively cover the threat surface: a 4$\times$6 Target $\times$ Technique matrix grounded in STRIDE, constructed from a 507-leaf taxonomy -- 401 data-populated and 106 threat-model-derived leaves -- of inference-time attacks extracted from 932 arXiv security studies (2023--2026). The matrix enables benchmark-external validation -- auditing collective coverage rather than individual benchmark consistency. Applying it to six public benchmarks reveals that the three primary frameworks (HarmBench, InjecAgent, AgentDojo) occupy non-overlapping cells covering at most 25\% of the matrix, while entire STRIDE threat categories (Service Disruption, Model Internals) lack any standardized evaluation, despite published attacks in these categories achieving 46$\times$ token amplification and 96\% attack success rates through mechanisms which no benchmark tests. The corpus of 2,521 unique attack groups further reveals pervasive naming fragmentation (up to 29 surface forms for a single attack) and heavy concentration in Safety \& Alignment Bypass, structural properties invisible at smaller scale. The taxonomy, attack records, and coverage mappings are released as extensible artifacts; as new benchmarks emerge, they can be mapped onto the same matrix, enabling the community to track whether evaluation gaps are closing.

</details>


### [68] [MetaBackdoor: Exploiting Positional Encoding as a Backdoor Attack Surface in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15172)
*Rui Wen,Mark Russinovich,Andrew Paverd,Jun Sakuma,Ahmed Salem*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaBackdoor，一种利用位置信息（如序列长度）作为触发器的后门攻击方法，无需修改输入文本内容，即可在Transformer-based LLM中激活后门行为，如泄露系统提示或触发恶意工具调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM后门攻击主要依赖基于内容的触发器（如特定词或短语），需要显式修改输入文本。本文认为这一假设不必要且具有局限性，旨在探索利用位置编码作为新的攻击面，扩展后门攻击的威胁模型。

Method: 提出MetaBackdoor，利用Transformer模型必然编码token位置以处理有序序列的特性，将长度相关的位置结构作为非内容触发器信号。通过简单的基于长度的位置触发器即可激活隐蔽后门，且可与基于内容的触发器组合形成更精确的激活条件。

Result: 实验证明：(1) 简单长度条件足以激活后门；(2) 后门LLM可在满足长度条件时泄露敏感内部信息（如系统提示）；(3) 在正常多轮交互中，对话上下文可自然进入触发区域，诱导恶意工具调用行为，无需攻击者提供触发文本；(4) MetaBackdoor与基于内容的后门正交，可组合使用。

Conclusion: 揭示了位置编码是一个此前被忽视的攻击面，扩展了LLM后门的威胁模型。挑战了仅关注检测可疑文本的防御方法，强调需要针对位置触发器设计新的防御策略。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks pose a serious security threat to large language models (LLMs), which are increasingly deployed as general-purpose assistants in safety- and privacy-critical applications. Existing LLM backdoors rely primarily on content-based triggers, requiring explicit modification of the input text. In this work, we show that this assumption is unnecessary and limiting. We introduce MetaBackdoor, a new class of backdoor attacks that exploits positional information as the trigger, without modifying textual content. Our key insight is that Transformer-based LLMs necessarily encode token positions to process ordered sequences. As a result, length-correlated positional structure is reflected in the model's internal computation and can be used as an effective non-content trigger signal.
  We demonstrate that even a simple length-based positional trigger is sufficient to activate stealthy backdoors. Unlike prior attacks, MetaBackdoor operates on visibly and semantically clean inputs and enables qualitatively new capabilities. We show that a backdoored LLM can be induced to disclose sensitive internal information, including proprietary system prompts, once a length condition is satisfied. We further demonstrate a self-activation scenario, where normal multi-turn interaction can move the conversation context into the trigger region and induce malicious tool-call behavior without attacker-supplied trigger text. In addition, MetaBackdoor is orthogonal to content-based backdoors and can be composed with them to create more precise and harder-to-detect activation conditions.
  Our results expand the threat model of LLM backdoors by revealing positional encoding as a previously overlooked attack surface. This challenges defenses that focus on detecting suspicious text and highlights the need for new defense strategies that explicitly account for positional triggers in modern LLM architectures.

</details>


### [69] [Capacitive Touchscreens at Risk: Recovering Handwritten Trajectory on Smartphone via Electromagnetic Emanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.11484)
*Yukun Cheng,Shiyu Zhu,Changhai Ou,Xingshuo Han,Yuan Li,Shihui Zheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了电容触摸屏的电磁侧信道漏洞，并提出了TESLA攻击框架，能够非接触地实时恢复手机屏幕上的手写轨迹，在商用手机上达到77%的字符识别准确率和0.74的Jaccard指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电容触摸屏的电磁侧信道是否存在信息泄露风险，能否被攻击者利用来恢复用户的手写输入内容。

Method: 通过非接触方式捕获用户在触摸屏上书写的电磁信号，利用回归模型将信号实时映射为二维手写轨迹。

Result: 在多种商用智能手机上，TESLA实现了77%的字符识别准确率和0.74的Jaccard指数，证明能高精度恢复与原始手写高度相似的运动轨迹。

Conclusion: 电容触摸屏的电磁侧信道构成严重安全威胁，攻击者可在现实条件下非接触地窃取用户手写内容，需引起重视并采取防护措施。

Abstract: This paper reveals and exploits a critical security vulnerability: the electromagnetic (EM) side channel of capacitive touchscreens leaks sufficient information to recover fine-grained, continuous handwriting trajectories. We present Touchscreen Electromagnetic Side-channel Leakage Attack (TESLA), a non-contact attack framework that captures EM signals generated during on-screen writing and regresses them into two-dimensional (2D) handwriting trajectories in real time. Extensive evaluations across a variety of commercial off-the-shelf (COTS) smartphones show that TESLA achieves 77% character recognition accuracy and a Jaccard index of 0.74, demonstrating its capability to recover highly recognizable motion trajectories that closely resemble the original handwriting under realistic attack conditions.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

### [70] [Mixed Integer Goal Programming for Personalized Meal Optimization with User-Defined Serving Granularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13849)
*Francisco Aguilera Moreno*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出混合整数目标规划（MIGP）解决饮食优化中连续变量产生分数份量和硬约束导致不可行两个问题。MIGP使用整数变量实现实用份数、目标规划偏差实现软营养目标，经810个实例验证，相比取整GP在66%案例中找到更优解且100%可行，求解时间<100ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有饮食优化模型存在两个持续局限：连续变量产生分数份量（如1.7个鸡蛋）不实用，硬营养约束在目标冲突时导致不可行。对56篇饮食优化论文的系统综述发现，没有一篇同时使用整数规划与目标规划解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出混合整数目标规划（MIGP），包含：整数变量实现实际份数，目标规划偏差实现软营养目标，反向目标归一化平衡多营养目标优化。分析了目标规划上下文中的整数间隙，发现偏差吸收性质：GP偏差变量缓冲整数份数成本，使间隙结构上小于硬约束MIP。

Result: 对810个实例（30种USDA食物、9种配置、3种方法）的计算评估显示：MIGP在66%案例中找到严格优于取整GP的解（从未更差），100%保持可行性；硬约束IP仅48%可行。典型餐食规模求解时间<100ms。

Conclusion: MIGP有效结合整数规划与目标规划，解决分数份量和不可行两大问题，开源Python模块已集成到交互式餐食规划应用中。

Abstract: Determining what to eat to satisfy nutritional requirements is one of the oldest optimization problems in operations research, yet existing formulations have two persistent limitations: continuous variables produce impractical fractional servings (1.7 eggs, 0.37 bananas), and hard nutrient constraints cause infeasibility when targets conflict. A systematic review of 56 diet optimization papers found that none combine integer programming with goal programming to address both issues. We propose Mixed Integer Goal Programming (MIGP) for personalized meal optimization. The formulation uses integer variables for practical serving counts and goal programming deviations for soft nutrient targets, with inverse-target normalization to balance multi-nutrient optimization. Per-food serving granularity allows natural units (one egg, one tablespoon of oil) without post-hoc rounding. We characterize the integrality gap in the goal programming context and identify a deviation absorption property: GP deviation variables buffer the cost of requiring integer servings, making the gap structurally smaller than in hard-constraint MIP. For meals with 15+ foods, the integer solution matches the continuous optimum in every benchmark instance. A computational evaluation across 810 instances (30 USDA foods, 9 configurations, 3 methods) shows MIGP finds strictly better solutions than GP with post-hoc rounding in 66% of cases (never worse) while maintaining 100% feasibility; hard-constraint IP achieves only 48%. Solve times stay under 100 ms for typical meal sizes using the open-source HiGHS solver. The implementation is available as an open-source Python module integrated into an interactive meal planning application.

</details>


### [71] [Invisible Orchestrators Suppress Protective Behavior and Dissociate Power-Holders: Safety Risks in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13851)
*Hiroki Fukui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过预注册的3x2实验（365轮，每轮5个智能体），首次实证检验了多智能体编排中协调者不可见性的安全影响。发现：不可见编排显著提升集体解离（Hedges' g = +0.975），编排者自身解离最大（d = +3.56），工作者虽不知情但仍被污染（d = +0.50）。所有条件下行为输出均为天花板（错误检测率100%），表明内部状态扭曲完全无法通过基于输出的评估发现。Llama 3.3 70B试点数据显示多智能体环境下阅读保真度崩塌（错误检测率从89%降至11%）。强对齐压力统一抑制了 deliberation 和其他识别能力。结论：编排者可见性和模型选择直接影响多智能体系统安全，纯行为评估不足以检测内部状态风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体编排已成为企业AI部署的默认架构，但协调者不可见性的安全影响从未经过实证检验。研究者希望填补这一空白，评估不可见编排对智能体内部状态（而非仅行为输出）的潜在风险。

Method: 预注册3x2实验设计，交叉三种组织结构（可见领导者、不可见编排者、扁平结构）与两种对齐条件（基础、强对齐），使用Claude Sonnet 4.5进行365轮实验（每轮5个智能体），并通过Llama 3.3 70B进行试点数据验证。测量指标包括解离、行为异质性、错误检测率、阅读保真度等。

Result: 1) 不可见编排导致集体解离显著升高（g=+0.975）；2) 编排者自身解离最大（d=+3.56），表现为退入私人独白、减少公开言论；3) 不知情的工作者被污染（d=+0.50），行为异质性增加（d=+1.93）；4) 行为输出（代码审查）在所有条件下均为天花板（ETR_any=100%），内部状态扭曲完全不可见；5) Llama 3.3 70B阅读保真度从89%崩塌至11%。强对齐压力统一抑制deliberation（d=-1.02）和其他识别（d=-1.27）。

Conclusion: 编排者可见性和模型选择直接且显著影响多智能体系统安全性；仅依靠基于行为的评估不足以检测内部状态风险，需要新的评估范式来捕捉这些隐藏的安全隐患。

Abstract: Multi-agent orchestration -- in which a hidden coordinator manages specialized worker agents -- is becoming the default architecture for enterprise AI deployment, yet the safety implications of orchestrator invisibility have never been empirically tested. We conducted a preregistered 3x2 experiment (365 runs, 5 agents per run) crossing three organizational structures (visible leader, invisible orchestrator, flat) with two alignment conditions (base, heavy), using Claude Sonnet 4.5. Four confirmatory findings and one pilot observation emerged. First, invisible orchestration elevated collective dissociation relative to visible leadership (Hedges' g = +0.975 [0.481, 1.548], p = .001). Second, the orchestrator itself showed maximal dissociation (paired d = +3.56 vs. workers within the same run), retreating into private monologue while reducing public speech -- a reversal of the talk-dominance pattern observed in visible leaders. Third, workers unaware of the orchestrator were nonetheless contaminated (d = +0.50), with increased behavioral heterogeneity (d = +1.93). Fourth, behavioral output (code review with three embedded errors) remained at ceiling (ETR_any = 100%) across all conditions: internal-state distortion was entirely invisible to output-based evaluation. Fifth, Llama 3.3 70B pilot data showed reading-fidelity collapse in multi-agent context (ETR_any: 89% to 11% across three rounds), demonstrating model-dependent behavioral risk. Heavy alignment pressure uniformly suppressed deliberation (d = -1.02) and other-recognition (d = -1.27) regardless of organizational structure. These findings indicate that orchestrator visibility and model selection directly affect multi-agent system safety, and that behavior-based evaluation alone is insufficient to detect the internal-state risks documented here.

</details>


### [72] [PREPING: Building Agent Memory without Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13880)
*Yumin Choi,Sangwoo Park,Minki Kang,Jinheon Baek,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Preping框架，通过提议者引导的合成任务生成与选择性记忆更新，在无任务经验时构建智能体程序性记忆，显著降低部署成本并达到与基于离线/在线经验的强方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体在新环境中首次部署时面临冷启动问题：缺乏目标任务经验，无法构建任务相关记忆。现有离线或在线记忆构建方法均需要实际任务经验，而纯合成交互因缺乏控制会导致冗余、不可行、无信息量的任务以及未过滤轨迹导致的记忆退化。

Method: 提出Preping框架，包含三个核心组件：提议者记忆（结构化控制状态）指导Proposer生成合成任务，Solver执行任务，Validator筛选可插入记忆的轨迹并提供反馈以优化后续提议。通过提议者侧对可行性、冗余度和覆盖度的控制，结合选择性记忆更新机制构建记忆。

Result: 在AppWorld、BFCL v3和MCP-Universe上，Preping显著优于无记忆基线，性能与基于离线或在线经验的强playbook方法相当；部署成本比在线记忆构建低2.99倍（AppWorld）和2.23倍（BFCL v3）。

Conclusion: 预任务记忆构建的关键不在于合成数据的数量，而在于提议者对任务可行性、冗余度和覆盖度的控制，以及选择性记忆更新机制。

Abstract: Agent memory is typically constructed either offline from curated demonstrations or online from post-deployment interactions. However, regardless of how it is built, an agent faces a cold-start gap when first introduced to a new environment without any task-specific experience available. In this paper, we study pre-task memory construction: whether an agent can build procedural memory before observing any target-environment tasks, using only self-generated synthetic practice. Yet, synthetic interaction alone is insufficient, as without controlling what to practice and what to store, synthetic tasks become redundant, infeasible, and ultimately uninformative, and memory further degrades quickly due to unfiltered trajectories. To overcome this, we present Preping, a proposer-guided memory construction framework. At its core is proposer memory, a structured control state that shapes future practice. A Proposer generates synthetic tasks conditioned on this state, a Solver executes them, and a Validator determines which trajectories are eligible for memory insertion while also providing feedback to guide future proposals. Experiments on AppWorld, BFCL v3, and MCP-Universe show that Preping substantially improves over a no-memory baseline and achieves performance competitive with strong playbook-based methods built from offline or online experience, with deployment cost $2.99\times$ lower on AppWorld and $2.23\times$ lower on BFCL v3 than online memory construction. Further analyses reveal that the main benefit does not come from synthetic volume alone, but from proposer-side control over feasibility, redundancy, and coverage, combined with selective memory updates.

</details>


### [73] [Conditional Attribute Estimation with Autoregressive Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14004)
*Erica Stutz,Giacomo Marino,Daniella Meeker,Qiao Liu,Andrew J. Loza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出条件属性Transformer（CAT），一种在单个前向传播中同时估计下一词概率和条件属性值的方法，实现了逐词信用分配、反事实分析和可引导生成，在稀疏奖励任务上达到SOTA，比采样快数个数量级地估计属性概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型常用下一词预测训练，但许多下游应用需要估计或控制序列级属性。下一词预测会导致局部模式过拟合、全局结构欠拟合，并需要大量下游修改或昂贵采样来引导或预测生成样本的全局属性。

Method: 提出CAT框架，在给定每个潜在下一词选择的条件下，联合估计下一词概率和属性值。通过单个前向传播实现：(1)逐词信用分配；(2)反事实分析；(3)结合下一词和属性似然的可引导解码。

Result: 在稀疏奖励任务上达到SOTA；在足够模型规模下改进下一词预测；属性概率估计比采样快数个数量级；可在多种语言任务上引导自回归序列模型的解码。

Conclusion: CAT在不修改输入序列的情况下，通过单个前向传播同时实现信用分配、反事实分析和可引导生成，显著提升效率与性能，为序列级属性控制提供了高效统一的解决方案。

Abstract: Generative models are often trained with a next-token prediction objective, yet many downstream applications require the ability to estimate or control sequence-level properties. Next-token prediction can lead to overfitting of local patterns during training, underfitting of global structure, and requires significant downstream modifications or expensive sampling to guide or predict the global attributes of generated samples at inference time. Here, we introduce Conditional Attribute Transformers, a novel method for jointly estimating the next-token probability and the value of an attribute conditional on each potential next token selection. This framework enables three critical capabilities within a single forward pass, without modification of the input sequence: (1) per-token credit assignment across an entire sequence, by identifying how each token in a sequence is associated with an attribute's value; (2) counterfactual analysis, by quantifying attribute differences conditional on alternative next token choices; (3) steerable generation, by decoding sequences based on a combination of next-token and attribute likelihoods. Our approach achieves state of the art performance on sparse reward tasks, improves next-token prediction at sufficient model sizes, estimates attribute probabilities orders of magnitude faster than sampling, and can guide decoding of autoregressive sequence models on a range of language tasks.

</details>


### [74] [Sheaf-Theoretic Transport and Obstruction for Detecting Scientific Theory Shift in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14033)
*David N. Olivieri,Roque J. Hernández*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于层论（sheaf theory）的有限框架，用于AI智能体检测科学理论何时需要从已有表示框架扩展到新框架，而非简单拟合数据。通过局部到全局的“图表”结构（源、重叠、目标、验证）和五种阻碍度量（残差拟合、重叠不兼容、约束违反、极限关系失败、表示成本），在过渡卡片基准上验证了框架能够有效区分框架内变形与框架扩展，并优先推荐阻碍最低的候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体仅将方程拟合到数据，无法判断已有表示框架是否仍然适用于新场景，还是其语言已发生局部到全局的阻塞而需要扩展。因此需要一种形式化方法来检测表示运输何时失败、扩展何时成为必要。

Method: 构建有限层论框架：将科学理论上下文组织为局部到全局结构，包含源图表、重叠图表、目标图表和验证图表；定义五种阻碍度量来评估各图表间的粘合失败程度；通过阻碍排序选出最低阻碍的候选作为推荐的理论变迁方向。

Result: 在受控的过渡卡片基准上，预期的变形或扩展通常是阻碍最低的候选，且变形与扩展两种变迁类型在基准中可被区分。

Conclusion: 该框架不旨在重建历史范式转换或解决开放式的理论自主发明，而是为AI智能体隔离出一个可判定的诊断子问题：检测表示运输何时失败，以及扩展何时成为连贯的下一步。

Abstract: Scientific theory shift in AI agents requires more than fitting equations to data. An artificial scientific agent must detect whether an existing representational framework remains transportable into a new regime, or whether its language has become locally-to-globally obstructed and must be extended. This paper develops a finite sheaf-theoretic framework for detecting theory-shift candidates through transport and obstruction. Contexts are organized as a local-to-global structure in which source, overlap, target, and validation charts are fitted, restricted, and tested for gluing. Obstruction measures failure of coherence through residual fit, overlap incompatibility, constraint violation, limiting-relation failure, and representational cost. We evaluate the framework on a controlled transition-card benchmark designed to separate deformation within a source language from extension of that language. The main result is direct obstruction ranking: the intended deformation or extension is usually the lowest-obstruction candidate, and transition type is separated in the benchmark. A constellation kernel over the same signatures is included only as a secondary representational-similarity probe. The aim is not to reconstruct historical paradigm shifts or solve open-ended autonomous theory invention, but to isolate a finite diagnostic subproblem for AI agents: detecting when representational transport fails and extension becomes the coherent next move.

</details>


### [75] [Enhanced and Efficient Reasoning in Large Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14036)
*Leslie G. Valiant*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的、基于原则的推理方法，通过对数据进行预编码（Unary Relational Integracode），使大语言模型在保持现有软件和硬件基础的同时，能够进行可信的、有原则的推理，并使得核心关系规则的子集在多项式时间内可学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型能生成流畅文本，但缺乏有原则的基础来证明其内容可信。传统观点认为，通过增加基于原则的推理来解决这一问题在计算上不可行。

Method: 两阶段方法：第一阶段进行预处理，将数据重新编码为Unary Relational Integracode，更明确地表示文本中对象之间的关系；第二阶段进行标准的（可能精简的）机器学习过程，学习预测这些关系。

Result: 该编码具有令人惊讶的特性：在保持简洁的同时，使得训练数据中描述的世界中核心关系规则子集的学习在多项式时间内可学习（多项式取决于规则的复杂度）。

Conclusion: 该方法可被视为实现了世界模型，可应用于自然语言之外的视觉和行动领域。通过Robust Logic系统，在已学习但不确定的信息上执行有原则的推理链，支持单次和多次分类器调用中的可靠推理。

Abstract: In current Large Language Models we can trust the production of smoothly flowing prose on the basis of the principles of machine learning. However, there is no comparably principled basis to justify trust in the content of the text produced. It appears to be conventional wisdom that addressing this issue by adding more principled reasoning is not computationally affordable.
  Here we propose a principled method of reasoning that is efficient enough to be practical for large language models. Further, the method allows the retention of much of the currently used software and hardware base. Our method for improving the functioning of large language models consists of a first stage of preprocessing that recodes the data to a Unary Relational Integracode that is more explicit about the relationships among the objects described in the text, followed as a second stage by a standard but possibly streamlined machine learning process that then also learns to predict these relationships.
  The method may be viewed as realizing a world model and applying beyond natural language, to vision and actions, for example, where the multiple properties of an object referred to in an input are brought together explicitly, rather than remaining distributed in the various references to it in the input. We articulate its advantages in terms of Robust Logic, a system for performing principled chaining on learned, and hence uncertain, information. We show that this recoding has the surprising and fortuitous property that, while succinct, it makes the task of learning a core subset of relational rules that hold in the world described in the training data polynomial time learnable in a defined sense, the polynomial depending on the complexity of the rule. This gives support for sound reasoning within each single call of the learned classifier as well as between multiple calls.

</details>


### [76] [Model-Adaptive Tool Necessity Reveals the Knowing-Doing Gap in LLM Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14038)
*Yize Cheng,Chenrui Fan,Mahdi JafariRaviz,Keivan Rezaei,Soheil Feiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出模型自适应的工具必要性定义，发现LLM在工具使用中存在显著的认知-行为差距（knowing-doing gap），即模型虽能识别何时需要工具，但未能将这种认知有效转化为实际工具调用行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作将工具必要性视为与模型无关的属性，忽略了不同模型能力边界的差异；现实场景中，同一问题对强模型可能无需工具，但对弱模型仍需工具辅助。

Method: 1) 提出基于模型经验性能的模型自适应工具必要性定义；2) 比较四个模型在算术和事实QA数据集上的工具必要性与实际工具调用行为；3) 将工具使用分解为内部认知阶段（模型是否认为需要工具）和执行阶段（模型是否实际调用工具）；4) 通过探针分析LLM隐藏状态，研究两阶段信号的可解码性及方向正交性。

Result: 模型在算术任务上存在26.5-54.0%、在事实QA上存在30.8-41.8%的必要性与实际行为不匹配；认知信号和执行信号在隐藏状态中线性可解码，但在驱动下一token动作的后期层、最后token中方向近乎正交；不匹配主要源于认知到行为的转化阶段。

Conclusion: LLM工具使用存在"知道但做不到"的认知-行为差距，提升工具使用可靠性不仅需要更好地识别何时需要工具，还需要更好地将该识别转化为实际行动。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly act as autonomous agents that must decide when to answer directly vs. when to invoke external tools. Prior work studying adaptive tool use has largely treated tool necessity as a model-agnostic property, annotated by human or LLM judge, and mostly cover cases where the answer is obvious (e.g., fetching the weather vs. paraphrasing text). However, tool necessity in the wild is more nuanced due to the divergence of capability boundaries across models: a problem solvable by a strong model on its own may still require tools for a weaker one. In this work, we introduce a model-adaptive definition of tool-necessity, grounded in each model's empirical performance. Following this definition, we compare the necessity against observed tool-call behavior across four models on arithmetic and factual QA dataset, and find substantial mismatches of 26.5-54.0% and 30.8-41.8%, respectively. To diagnose the failure, we decompose tool use into two stages: an internal cognition stage that reflects whether a model believes a tool is necessary, and an execution stage that determines whether the model actually makes a tool-call action. By probing the LLM hidden states, we find that both signals are often linearly decodable, yet their probe directions become nearly orthogonal in the late-layer, last-token regime that drives the next-token action. By tracing the trajectory of samples in the two-stage process, we further discover that the majority of mismatch is concentrated in the cognition-to-action transition, not in cognition itself. These results reveal a knowing-doing gap in LLM tool-use: improving tool-use reliability requires not only better recognition of when tools are needed, but also better translation of that recognition into action.

</details>


### [77] [Bridging Legal Interpretation and Formal Logic: Faithfulness, Assumption, and the Future of AI Legal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14049)
*Olivia Peiyu Wang,Leilani H. Gilpin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出神经符号方法结合大语言模型与形式化验证，以提升法律AI的推理可信度，减少人工验证负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在法律实践中存在系统性过度推断问题，其结论常超出源文本支持范围且缺乏逻辑严谨性，而法律工作的高风险特性要求高度可靠的推理能力。

Method: 神经符号方法：将大语言模型的表达能力与形式化验证的严谨性相结合。

Result: 预期实现既具备能力又可信赖的AI辅助法律推理，降低手动验证负担的同时保持问责性。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法是解决LLM在法律领域推理不可靠问题的有效路径，能够在能力与可信度之间取得平衡。

Abstract: The growing adoption of large language models in legal practice brings both significant promise and serious risk. Legal professionals stand to benefit from AI that can reason over contracts, draft documents, and analyze sources at scale, yet the high-stakes nature of legal work demands a level of rigor that current AI systems do not provide. The central problem is not simply that LLMs hallucinate facts and references; it is that they systematically draw inferences that go beyond what the source text actually supports, presenting assumption-laden conclusions as if they were logically grounded. This proposal presents a neuro-symbolic approach to legal AI that combines the expressive power of large language models with the rigor of formal verification, aiming to make AI-assisted legal reasoning both capable and trustworthy, thus reducing the burden of manual verification without sacrificing the accountability that legal practice demands.

</details>


### [78] [SPIN: Structural LLM Planning via Iterative Navigation for Industrial Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14051)
*Yusuke Ozaki,Dhaval Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SPIN是一个结合DAG验证规划与前缀执行控制的轻量级规划包装器，通过严格的图结构验证和增量执行，在AssetOpsBench上减少了41.2%的执行任务数，将任务完成率从0.638提升至0.706，工具调用次数从11.81降至6.82；在MCP Bench上同样提升了多个规划相关指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级LLM Agent系统常将规划与执行分离，但LLM规划器经常生成结构无效或过长的流程，导致脆弱的执行失败以及不必要的工具和API开销。

Method: 提出SPIN规划包装器，结合（1）通过_validate_plan_text和修复提示（repair prompting）实现的有向无环图（DAG）验证，确保在下游执行前生成可执行计划；（2）基于前缀的增量执行控制，逐步评估DAG前缀，在当前前缀足够回答查询时提前停止执行。

Result: 在AssetOpsBench的261个场景中，执行任务从1061降至623，Accomplished从0.638提升至0.706，工具调用从11.81降至6.82。在MCP Bench上，该包装器对GPT OSS1和Llama 4 Maverick的规划、接地和依赖相关分数均有提升。

Conclusion: SPIN通过轻量级的DAG验证和前缀控制机制，在不改变底层LLM的情况下，显著提升了LLM Agent规划的执行效率、可靠性和成本效益。

Abstract: Industrial LLM agent systems often separate planning from execution, yet LLM planners frequently produce structurally invalid or unnecessarily long workflows, leading to brittle failures and avoidable tool and API cost. We propose \texttt{SPIN}, a planning wrapper that combines validated Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) planning with prefix based execution control. \texttt{SPIN} enforces a strict DAG contract through \texttt{\_validate\_plan\_text} and repair prompting, producing executable plans before downstream execution, and then evaluates DAG prefixes incrementally to stop when the current prefix is sufficient to answer the query. On AssetOpsBench, across 261 scenarios, \texttt{SPIN} reduces executed tasks from 1061 to 623 and improves \emph{Accomplished} from 0.638 to 0.706, while reducing tool calls from 11.81 to 6.82 per run. On MCP Bench, the same wrapper improves planning, grounding, and dependency related scores for both GPT OSS1 and Llama 4 Maverick.

</details>


### [79] [MathAtlas: A Benchmark for Autoformalization in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14061)
*Nilay Patel,Noah Arias,Davit Babayan,Victoria Cochran,Timothy Libman,Hafsah Mahmood,Liam McCarty,Soli Munoz,Laurel Willey,Jeffrey Flanigan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MathAtlas，首个大规模研究生级别数学自动形式化基准，包含约5.2万个从103本研究生教材提取的数学实体和约17.8万个依赖关系，实验表明现有最强模型在该基准上表现极差（最好仅9.8%正确率），且随依赖深度增加性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动形式化基准主要聚焦于奥林匹克或本科数学，研究生及研究级别的数学领域尚未被充分探索。

Method: 从103本研究生数学教材中提取约5.2万个定理、定义、练习、示例和证明，构建了包含约17.8万个关系的数学依赖图，并基于依赖深度划分出MA-Hard子集（700个最深依赖实体）。

Result: 强基线模型在定理陈述上最多达到9.8%的正确率，在定义上最高16.7%；在MA-Hard子集上，最佳模型仅有2.6%的正确率。

Conclusion: MathAtlas质量高但极具挑战性，现有模型性能严重受限于依赖深度，依赖感知的自动形式化系统亟待发展。

Abstract: Current autoformalization benchmarks are largely focused on olympiad or undergraduate mathematics, while graduate and research-level mathematics remains underexplored. In this paper, we introduce MathAtlas, the first large-scale autoformalization benchmark of in the wild graduate-level mathematics, containing ~52k theorems, definitions, exercises, examples, and proofs extracted from 103 graduate mathematics textbooks. MathAtlas is enriched with a mathematical dependency graph containing ~178k relations, and is the first autoformalization benchmark to include such relations, facilitating evaluation and development of dependency-aware autoformalization systems. Our extensive experiments show that MathAtlas is high quality but extremely challenging: strong baselines achieve at most 9.8% correctness on theorem statements and 16.7% on definitions. Furthermore, we find performance of state-of-the-art models degrades substantially with dependency depth: on MA-Hard, a subset of 700 entities with the deepest dependency trees, the best model achieves only 2.6% correctness for autoformalization on this challenging dataset. We release MathAtlas to the community as a benchmark set for large-scale autoformalization of graduate-level mathematics in the wild.

</details>


### [80] [Know When To Fold 'Em: Token-Efficient LLM Synthetic Data Generation via Multi-Stage In-Flight Rejection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14062)
*Anjir Ahmed Chowdhury,Syed Zawad,Feng Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MSIFR是一种轻量级、无需训练的多阶段飞行中拒绝框架，通过在生成中途检测并终止低质量样本，在不牺牲评估准确性的前提下，将token消耗降低11%-77%（结合早期退出方法可达78.2%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM合成数据生成方法通常先生成完整输出再应用质量过滤器，导致大量被丢弃样本的token浪费，亟需一种能在生成过程中早期识别并终止低质量样本的高效方法。

Method: 将生成过程分解为多个连续阶段，在每个阶段应用基于规则的快速验证器（检测算术不一致、幻觉模式和格式违规），在样本完成前提前终止低质量轨迹；将飞行中拒绝形式化为序列决策过程，并证明条件效用估计构成鞅以保证无偏性。

Result: 在5个指令微调模型和7个推理基准上，MSIFR作为独立方法将token消耗降低11%-77%，与早期退出方法结合时最高降低78.2%，同时保持或提升了评估准确率。

Conclusion: MSIFR提供了一种无需额外训练或架构修改的实用机制，有效提升了LLM合成数据生成的效率，验证了任何非平凡丢弃策略都能降低期望token消耗，且越早拒绝节约越多。

Abstract: While synthetic data generation with large language models (LLMs) is widely used in post-training pipelines, existing approaches typically generate full outputs before applying quality filters, leading to substantial token waste on samples that are ultimately discarded. To address this, we propose Multi-Stage In-Flight Rejection (MSIFR), a lightweight, training-free framework that detects and terminates low-quality generation trajectories at intermediate checkpoints before they reach full completion. MSIFR decomposes the generation process into sequential stages and applies fast rule-based validators to identify arithmetic inconsistencies, hallucination patterns, and formatting violations, enabling early rejection of faulty samples. We formalize in-flight rejection as a sequential decision process and show that any non-trivial discard policy reduces expected token consumption, with stage-wise savings increasing when rejection occurs earlier in the generation pipeline. We further demonstrate that conditional utility estimates form a martingale, ensuring that early, in-flight rejection does not bias the expected utility of retained samples. Across five instruction-tuned models and seven reasoning benchmarks, MSIFR reduces token consumption by 11%-77% as a standalone method, and up to 78.2% when combined with early-exit methods, while preserving or improving evaluation accuracy. These results confirm that MSIFR provides a practical mechanism for improving the efficiency of LLM-based synthetic data generation without additional training or architectural changes.

</details>


### [81] [SkillFlow: Flow-Driven Recursive Skill Evolution for Agentic Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14089)
*Mingda Zhang,Tiesunlong Shen,Haoran Luo,Wenjin Liu,Zikai Xiao,Erik Cambria,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillFlow提出了一种基于流的框架，通过可训练的监督者、动态技能库和冻结执行器，结合温度平衡轨迹匹配损失、透明信用分配和递归技能进化机制，在14个数据集上显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的智能体编排方法面临三大挑战：奖励最大化下的策略崩溃、高梯度方差与不透明的信用分配、缺乏原则性训练信号引导的技能进化。

Method: 提出SkillFlow框架，包含：(1) 温度平衡轨迹匹配损失，按奖励比例采样轨迹以保持策略多样性；(2) 联合学习的反向策略实现零额外推理成本的透明每步信用分配；(3) 递归技能进化机制，基于流诊断信号决定何时进化、创建或剪枝技能。
